Yeah, um… I guess what brought me in is that I’ve been kind of… taking on a lot. Maybe too much.

I don’t know if that’s the kind of thing you mean, but… it started feeling like maybe I should talk to someone.

More like I was really fired up, like everything made sense and I could handle way more than usual. But yeah… if I’m being honest, that fired-up, overconfident feeling lasted maybe two weeks before I sort of crashed.\n\nDoctor: Have you had periods of feeling really down followed by periods of feeling fired up like this before?\n\nPatient: I’m not totally sure, honestly. I mean, I’ve had ups and downs like anyone, I guess, but I don’t know if I’ve noticed this exact pattern before… like a real low and then that fired-up feeling after.\n\nMaybe I didn’t pay enough attention, or maybe I just wrote it off as stress. I’m not sure I’ve really thought about them as connected before.\n\nPart of me wants to say it’s just stress or me trying to catch up after being in a bad place, but… I guess it could be related somehow. I don’t want to overstate it, though. I’m not totally sure what I’m looking at. Does that answer make sense?\n\nDoctor: After you crashed from feeling fired up, did you go back to feeling normal or did you go into a depressed state?\n\nPatient: I… I don’t think I went back to normal, no. I snapped at a coworker pretty hard, more than was fair. I’m not proud of that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt really down or depressed for a period of time?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I have. I mean, I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is, but it’s been kind of hanging over me for… maybe about a year now.\n\nAnd this latest crash, or whatever you’d call it, has been the worst it’s gotten. I probably should’ve said that sooner.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this fired up and able to handle more than usual?\n\nPatient: Um… I think it was about two weeks. It started around when I got really into trying to remake myself for that park pitch, like I was suddenly the guy who could pull it all together.\n\nAt first it seemed like a good thing, you know? Like motivation. But yeah… if I’m being honest, that fired-up, overconfident feeling lasted maybe two weeks before I sort of crashed.\n\nDoctor: Have you had periods of feeling really down followed by periods of feeling fired up like this before?\n\nPatient: I’m not totally sure, honestly. I mean, I’ve had ups and downs like anyone, I guess, but I don’t know if I’ve noticed this exact pattern before… like a real low and then that fired-up feeling after.\n\nMaybe I didn’t pay enough attention, or maybe I just wrote it off as stress. Does that make sense?\n\nDoctor: Do you think your periods of feeling down and your periods of feeling fired up might be connected in some way?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… maybe? I’m not sure I’ve really thought about them as connected before.\n\nPart of me wants to say it’s just stress or me trying to catch up after being in a bad place, but… I guess it could be related somehow. I don’t want to overstate it, though. I’m not totally sure what I’m looking at. Does that answer make sense?\n\nDoctor: After you crashed from feeling fired up, did you go back to feeling normal or did you go into a depressed state?\n\nPatient: I… I don’t think I went back to normal, no. I kind of wish I could say that.\n\nThe low feeling has honestly been hanging over me for… about a year, I guess. Before I crashed, I had this stretch where I felt really fired up, like… almost bigger than myself, if that makes sense.\n\nI don’t want to make it sound like I was out of control or anything, but I was definitely not my usual self. I haven’t tracked it closely enough to say there’s a pattern like that.\n\nIt might happen sometimes, like maybe after I’ve been down I push myself hard to get back on top of things, but I don’t know if that’s the same as “fired up.” I don’t want to give you a wrong answer just to sound certain. Does that make sense?\n\nDoctor: Do you usually feel fired up after a period of feeling down?\n\nPatient: I guess… I don’t really know. I’m sorry, I know I kind of answered that already, but I haven’t noticed a clear “down first, then fired up” pattern.\n\nSometimes I’m already feeling low and I push myself to get moving, but I don’t know if that turns into that fired-up feeling or if I’m just trying not to fall behind. I don’t want to give you the wrong impression just to sound sure.\n\nDoctor: When you're feeling down, do you ever have moments where you feel fired up at the same time?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think maybe sometimes, but it’s hard to explain right.\n\nLike before I crashed, I remember feeling really fired up—almost like bigger than myself, if that makes sense. Almost… bigger than myself, I guess, if that doesn’t sound too dramatic.\n\nBut I wasn’t just cheerful. If someone at work questioned the plan or told me to slow down, I’d get irritated fast. I snapped at a coworker pretty hard, more than was fair. I’m not proud of that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt really down or depressed for a period of time?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I have. I mean, I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is, but it’s been kind of hanging over me for… maybe about a year now.\n\nAnd this latest crash, or whatever you’d call it, has been the worst it’s gotten. I probably should’ve said that sooner.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this fired up and able to handle more than usual?\n\nPatient: Um… I think it was about two weeks. It started around when I got really into trying to remake myself for that park pitch, like I was suddenly the guy who could pull it all together.\n\nAt first it seemed like a good thing, you know? Like motivation. But yeah… if I’m being honest, that fired-up, overconfident feeling lasted maybe two weeks before I sort of crashed.\n\nDoctor: Have you had periods of feeling really down followed by periods of feeling fired up like this before?\n\nPatient: I’m not totally sure, honestly. I mean, I’ve had ups and downs like anyone, I guess, but I don’t know if I’ve noticed this exact pattern before… like a real low and then that fired-up feeling after.\n\nMaybe I didn’t pay enough attention, or maybe I just wrote it off as stress. Does that make sense?\n\nDoctor: Do you think your periods of feeling down and your periods of feeling fired up might be connected in some way?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… maybe? I’m not sure I’ve really thought about them as connected before.\n\nPart of me wants to say it’s just stress or me trying to catch up after being in a bad place, but… I guess it could be related somehow. I don’t want to overstate it, though. I’m not totally sure what I’m looking at. Does that answer make sense?\n\nDoctor: After you crashed from feeling fired up, did you go back to feeling normal or did you go into a depressed state?\n\nPatient: I… I don’t think I went back to normal, no. I kind of wish I could say that.\n\nThe low feeling has honestly been hanging over me for… about a year, I guess. And after that fired-up stretch ended, it was more like I dropped back into it — maybe worse than before. This latest crash has probably been the worst it’s gotten.\n\nI’m sorry, I don’t want to make it sound dramatic, but that’s the honest answer.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of feeling abnormally happy or irritable that lasted for more than a few days?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so. Before I crashed, I had this stretch where I felt really fired up, like… almost bigger than myself, if that makes sense.\n\nI don’t want to make it sound like I was out of control or anything, but I was definitely not my usual self. And I got irritated pretty fast. Like if a coworker questioned my plan or told me to slow down, I’d snap at him way harder than it deserved.\n\nIt lasted more than a couple days, yeah. Does that count as what you mean?\n\nDoctor: Do you usually start feeling fired up after a period of feeling down?\n\nPatient: I’m not totally sure, honestly. I haven’t tracked it closely enough to say there’s a pattern like that.\n\nIt might happen sometimes, like maybe after I’ve been down I push myself hard to get back on top of things, but I don’t know if that’s the same as “fired up.” I don’t want to give you a wrong answer just to sound certain. Does that make sense?\n\nDoctor: Do you usually feel fired up after a period of feeling down?\n\nPatient: I guess… I don’t really know. I’m sorry, I know I kind of answered that already, but I haven’t noticed a clear “down first, then fired up” pattern.\n\nSometimes I’m already feeling low and I push myself to get moving, but I don’t know if that turns into that fired-up feeling or if I’m just trying not to fall behind. I don’t want to say yes if I’m not sure.\n\nDoctor: Do you think you've had more than one episode of feeling fired up like this in your life?\n\nPatient: I’m not completely sure. I mean… maybe? There have been times where I felt more energized or driven than usual, but I don’t know if they were the same kind of thing or if I’m just looking back and trying to make it fit.\n\nI don’t want to overstate it. I’d say there’s been at least that one stretch I mentioned, and maybe others, but I’m not confident enough to say for sure. Is that an okay way to answer?\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that your periods of feeling down and your periods of feeling fired up tend to happen in a cycle, with one following the other in a regular pattern?\n\nPatient: I don’t think I’ve noticed a regular cycle like that, no. Not in a way where I could say, “first I’m down, then I’m fired up,” over and over.\n\nIt feels more messy than that to me, or maybe I just haven’t been paying close enough attention. I’m sorry—I know that’s not a very clear answer, but I don’t want to make a pattern out of it if I’m not sure it’s really there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your periods of feeling down and feeling fired up happen in some kind of repeating pattern?\n\nPatient: I honestly don’t know that I can say it repeats in a pattern. I wish I had a cleaner answer for you.\n\nIt’s more like… I notice the down times more clearly, and then other times I’m more up or driven, but I haven’t tracked it enough to say they take turns or happen on a schedule. I don’t want to give you the wrong impression just to sound sure.\n\nDoctor: When you're feeling down, do you ever have moments where you feel fired up at the same time?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think maybe sometimes, but it’s hard to explain right.\n\nLike before I crashed, I remember feeling really fired up—almost like bigger than myself, if that makes sense. I don’t mean that in some dramatic way, just… really pushed forward. But at the same time I could get pretty irritable. If a coworker questioned my plan or told me to slow down, I’d snap at him harder than I should have.\n\nI’m not proud of that. I don’t know if that’s the kind of “at the same time” you mean, but that did happen.\n\nDoctor: When you're feeling fired up, do you still have moments where you feel down?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. When I’m fired up, I usually think of it as me doing better, or at least being more productive, so I don’t always notice feeling down at the same time.\n\nMaybe there are little dips, but I don’t know if I’d call it feeling down exactly. I don’t want to overstate it. Is that the kind of thing you mean?\n\nDoctor: When you're feeling fired up, do you still experience any of the feelings you have when you're feeling down?\n\nPatient: I don’t really know. I haven’t noticed it clearly, I guess.\n\nWhen I’m fired up, I tend to focus on the fact that I’m moving and getting things done, so I don’t always check in with whether any of the “down” feelings are still there. Maybe there’s some of it underneath, but I wouldn’t want to say yes if I’m not sure. Does that make sense?\n\nDoctor: How long do your periods of feeling down usually last?\n\nPatient: It’s been… kind of a long time, honestly. I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is, but it feels like it’s been hanging over me for about a year.\n\nAnd this latest crash, if I’m calling it that, has been the worst it’s gotten. Does that answer what you mean by how long?\n\nDoctor: Do you usually start feeling fired up right after a period of feeling down?\n\nPatient: I’m not totally sure there’s a clear pattern like that. Sometimes it can feel like after I’ve been down, I sort of try to pull myself out of it and get moving, but I don’t know if I’d say it usually happens right after.\n\nI might not be tracking it well enough, honestly. I’m not sure I’ve really thought about them as connected before.\n\nPart of me wants to say it’s just stress or me trying to catch up after being in a bad place, but… I guess it could be related somehow. I don’t want to overstate it, though. I’m not totally sure what I’m looking at. Does that answer make sense?\n\nDoctor: After you crashed from feeling fired up, did you go back to feeling normal or did you go into a depressed state?\n\nPatient: I… I don’t think I went back to normal, no. I kind of wish I could say that.\n\nThe low feeling has honestly been hanging over me for… about a year, I guess. And after that fired-up stretch ended, it was more like I dropped back into it — maybe worse than before. This latest crash has probably been the worst it’s gotten.\n\nI’m sorry, I don’t want to make it sound dramatic, but that’s the honest answer.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of feeling abnormally happy or irritable that lasted for more than a few days?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so. Before I crashed, I had this stretch where I felt really fired up, like… almost bigger than myself, if that makes sense.\n\nI don’t want to make it sound like I was out of control or anything, but I was definitely not my usual self. And I got irritated pretty fast. Like if a coworker questioned my plan or told me to slow down, I’d snap at him way harder than it deserved.\n\nIt lasted more than a couple days, yeah. Does that count as what you mean?\n\nDoctor: Do you usually start feeling fired up after a period of feeling down?\n\nPatient: I’m not totally sure, honestly. I haven’t tracked it closely enough to say there’s a pattern like that.\n\nIt might happen sometimes, like maybe after I’ve been down I push myself hard to get back on top of things, but I don’t know if that’s the same as “fired up.” I don’t want to give you a wrong answer just to sound certain. Does that make sense?\n\nDoctor: Do you usually feel fired up after a period of feeling down?\n\nPatient: I guess… I don’t really know. I’m sorry, I know I kind of answered that already, but I haven’t noticed a clear “down first, then fired up” pattern.\n\nSometimes I’m already feeling low and I push myself to get moving, but I don’t know if that turns into that fired-up feeling or if I’m just trying not to fall behind. I don’t want to say yes if I’m not sure.\n\nDoctor: Do you think you've had more than one episode of feeling fired up like this in your life?\n\nPatient: I’m not completely sure. I mean… maybe? There have been times where I felt more energized or driven than usual, but I don’t know if they were the same kind of thing or if I’m just looking back and trying to make it fit.\n\nI don’t want to overstate it. I’d say there’s been at least that one stretch I mentioned, and maybe others, but I’m not confident enough to say for sure. Is that an okay way to answer?\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that your periods of feeling down and your periods of feeling fired up tend to happen in a cycle, with one following the other in a regular pattern?\n\nPatient: I don’t think I’ve noticed a regular cycle like that, no. Not in a way where I could say, “first I’m down, then I’m fired up,” over and over.\n\nIt feels more messy than that to me, or maybe I just haven’t been paying close enough attention. I’m sorry—I know that’s not a very clear answer, but I don’t want to make a pattern out of it if I’m not sure it’s really there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your periods of feeling down and feeling fired up happen in some kind of repeating pattern?\n\nPatient: I honestly don’t know that I can say it repeats in a pattern. I wish I had a cleaner answer for you.\n\nIt’s more like… I notice the down times more clearly, and then other times I’m more up or driven, but I haven’t tracked it enough to say they take turns or happen on a schedule. I don’t want to give you the wrong impression just to sound sure.\n\nDoctor: When you're feeling down, do you ever have moments where you feel fired up at the same time?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think maybe sometimes, but it’s hard to explain right.\n\nLike before I crashed, I remember feeling really fired up—almost like bigger than myself, if that makes sense. I don’t mean that in some dramatic way, just… really pushed forward. But at the same time I could get pretty irritable. If a coworker questioned my plan or told me to slow down, I’d snap at him harder than I should have.\n\nI’m not proud of that. I don’t know if that’s the kind of “at the same time” you mean, but that did happen.\n\nDoctor: When you're feeling fired up, do you still have moments where you feel down?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. When I’m fired up, I usually think of it as me doing better, or at least being more productive, so I don’t always notice feeling down at the same time.\n\nMaybe there are little dips, but I don’t know if I’d call it feeling down exactly. I don’t want to overstate it. Is that the kind of thing you mean?\n\nDoctor: When you're feeling fired up, do you still experience any of the feelings you have when you're feeling down?\n\nPatient: I don’t really know. I haven’t noticed it clearly, I guess.\n\nWhen I’m fired up, I tend to focus on the fact that I’m moving and getting things done, so I don’t always check in with whether any of the “down” feelings are still there. Maybe there’s some of it underneath, but I wouldn’t want to say yes if I’m not sure. Does that make sense?\n\nDoctor: How long do your periods of feeling down usually last?\n\nPatient: It’s been… kind of a long time, honestly. I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is, but it feels like it’s been hanging over me for about a year.\n\nAnd this latest crash, if I’m calling it that, has been the worst it’s gotten. Does that answer what you mean by how long?\n\nDoctor: Do you usually start feeling fired up right after a period of feeling down?\n\nPatient: I’m not totally sure there’s a clear pattern like that.
